# STATUS.md

Current progress snapshot for this project. This file changes often —
update it as part of the same commit whenever the plan advances (a day
file's tasks get checked off, a phase starts or completes, a build/test
command changes). See `CLAUDE.md` for the durable rules that don't change
with progress.

Failing tests, flaky tests and deferred items are tracked in `KNOWN-ISSUES.md`.

**Phase 28 Day 502 done (2026-11-24, the Windows PC): Direct3D 12 for the phase's features.** Cube textures on Direct3D 12 (a 6-slice array with a `TEXTURECUBE` view, `Caps::cube_textures` true); DXIL minted for `lighting.frag` (the environment cube) and the five `gbuffer.vert` modules; the terrain tile buffer is a read-only structured buffer with its stride, so the terrain draws on Direct3D 12; instanced sets and trees draw there through the object index stream, now 262,144 entries long, and no frame writes an instance past it. The terrain golden scene equals Linux's (step 600 `0x447cc3f9738ad539`). Four trees build clean. `--affected` plain: `ran 639 of 8368 tests (phases: 3 4 5 23 28; flavors: plain; ...) in 56 s` (only `docs_check` failed, before the reference was regenerated); asan: `ran 523 of 8368 tests (...) in 65 s`, none failed; `ctest -L docs` 12 of 12. Review: 3 findings, all fixed (two messages, a test of the stream cap). Open for the Mac: `gbuffer.vert`'s Metal library (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). **Next: Day 504 (the render-features demo and benchmarks).**

**The PC after Phase 28 Days 478-494 and the double precision series (2026-11-23):** the four Windows trees build clean after MSVC's `f64`-to-`f32` warnings (about 40 explicit casts in the physics demo and the spatial, physics, script-physics and C API tests; `SameBits` got an `f64` overload) and a unity clash in `cube_texture_test.cpp`. DXIL minted for `cube_sample.comp`, `sky.frag`, `sky_cube.frag`, `terrain.vert` and `terrain.frag` (its register fixed, `b1` to `b3`); `lighting.frag`'s DXIL kept as committed until Day 502 gives Direct3D 12 cube textures (the re-minted one broke every Direct3D 12 lighting pipeline). Fixed: `TerrainPass` leaked its objects at a device reset and freed a texture on the destroyed device when its `Build` failed half-way (crash in a renderer reset test). `renderer_tests`, `rhi_tests` and `scene_tests` now install the crash handler (`tests/support/crash_handler_listener.cpp`; dumps under `test-tmp/crash-dumps/<target>`), so a crash or `abort()` writes a report and a minidump instead of a dialog that stops the run. Plain `build-msvc` over physics, spatial, rhi, renderer, scene, engine_library, shaders, asset, script_physics, boundary and docs (60 s per test): 1705 of 1720; the 15 are Day 502's (terrain on Direct3D 12, the lighting DXIL) and one Debug test over the cap (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Not run: asan, clang-cl, `--affected`.

**`entity.find` and `entity.name` in a C API world (maintenance fix, 2026-11-22; `plans/maintenance/script-entity-find.md`).** They returned nil in every C API world because `engine_c` never set the script runtime's `SceneServices`; it does now (`SceneNames`), over the names `entity_name_set` and `level_spawn` write. `host_script_scene` covers a level node and a host-named entity, moved by a script and read back. Scoped runs (phases 23 27): plain `ran 396 of 11716`, asan `ran 334 of 11716`, tsan `ran 331 of 11716`, no failures; not built on the PC or the Mac.

**Double precision, Day 1 done (2026-11-23): design spec and spikes** (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-23-double-precision-design.md`). Picks: the math types become templates on the scalar with `Vec3 = Vec3T<f32>` and `Vec3d = Vec3T<f64>` (the user's choice; compile time is the same as separate types) plus a plain `Placement`; physics keeps `f64` positions and broad phase with body-relative `f32` contacts and solver (kernels show equal arithmetic cost; real cost measured on Days 6-7); the scene is stored and culled in `f64` and subtracted where GPU data is written (0.43 ms for 100,000 objects); formats and the C API grow by new versions and sized structs. No code kept.

**Double precision, Day 2 done (2026-11-23, Linux): the foundation math types.** `Vec2T/Vec3T/Vec4T`, `SphereT`, `AabbT`, `BoundsT`, `PlaneT`, `RayT` and `FrustumT` are templates on the scalar in `math/vector.h` and `math/geometry.h`; the old names are `f32` aliases (no other file in `engine/`, `game/`, `tools/` or `tests/` changed to compile) and `Vec3d`, `Aabbd`, `Sphered`, `Boundsd`, `Planed`, `Rayd`, `Frustumd` are new. Widening is implicit and exact, narrowing is the named `Narrow()`; `FrustumT::Translated(origin)` moves a camera-space frustum into world space; `Placement` (`math/placement.h`) is an `f32` linear block plus a `Vec3d` position with `RelativeTo(origin)`. 7 new test cases (10,000-value reference, 10,000 km millimetre test, `Placement`, f64 frustum, sphere, plane and bounds). Builds clean: clang unity, GCC unity-off. Scoped runs: plain `ran 4482 of 11993 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 154 s`, 5 failures, all known (DXIL and Metal minted libraries, voice-cost snapshot in a Debug tree, two X11 window tests); asan `ran 3670 of 11993 tests (phases: all; flavors: asan; ...) in 181 s`, 2 failures (the two minted libraries). Reduced runs; tsan not run (no concurrency code). Also fixed stale expectations left by Phase 28 Day 493 (three demo `listsettings.golden` files, the renderer settings count 15 to 18, a float default compared as text). `/code-review high`: 2 findings, 1 fixed (f64 sphere/plane/bounds test), 1 declined (day file). **Next: Day 3 (ECS transforms, when you say).**

**Double precision, Day 3 done (2026-11-23, Linux): entity transforms.** `ecs::LocalTransform::position` is a `Vec3d`; `WorldTransform` gains an exact `Vec3d position` beside `matrix` (whose translation is that position narrowed) with `FromMatrix`, `FromPlacement` and `ToPlacement`; the propagation composes the translation in `f64` (`ComposeWorld`) and the rotation and scale in `f32` as before (100,000-entity hierarchy bench in a Release tree: 5.2 ms, no slower than before). Storage: `FieldKind::Vec3d` (binary, SQL columns, the level text form, script component bindings read it as a narrowed `vec3`); `local_transform` is stored version 2 and the oldest 2 (older stores and levels are refused until re-minted). Readers that need single precision (scripts, physics sync, the C API, spatial, renderer sync) narrow with a named `.Narrow()` or read `matrix`; each is the later day of the series (KNOWN-ISSUES). New tests: a five-deep chain 10,000 km away keeps its millimetres exactly, a save and load of a 1,000 km entity is exact; two stale tests adapted (ten-parent chain compares the rotation and scale exactly and the translation within rounding; a test-local `Placement` renamed). Builds clean: clang unity, GCC unity-off. Runs: plain `ran 2413 of 12000 tests (phases: 3 4 5 17 18 19 22 23 25 27 28; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 68 s`, no failure; asan `ran 2204 of 12000 tests (... flavors: asan ...) in 177 s`, no failure; tsan `ran 1896 of 12000 tests (... flavors: tsan ...) in 246 s`, 1 failure (the known LuaJIT sandbox flake); a plain `--all` (`ran 4678 of 12000 tests`) failed only the known DXIL, Metal and voice-cost tests. Reduced runs. `/code-review high`: 3 findings, 1 fixed, 2 recorded. **Next: Day 4 (camera-relative renderer, when you say).**

**Double precision, Day 4 done (2026-11-23, Linux): the renderer follows a floating render origin.** Design change recorded in the spec ("Amendment (Day 4)"): instead of an `f64` scene, the scene stays `f32` and holds values relative to `scene::SceneLink::origin`; `scene::UpdateRenderOrigin(link, camera)` re-bases it (snapped to `rebase_step`, 0 = off, the default) and the sync rewrites objects, lights and terrains, subtracting in `f64` (`scene::RelativeMatrix`); the camera system's camera is relative too. No change when the step is 0. Tests: a scene at (1e8, 2e7, -5e7) renders within 3 levels of the same scene at the origin on every GPU backend that exists here (fails without the origin), objects 1 mm apart stay distinct, the re-base rewrites matrices. Guide: "Large worlds" in `docs/guide/renderer.md`. Not done from the day file: `renderer::Camera` keeps an `f32` (relative) position and the instance sets do not exist yet (paused Phase 28 days take the origin). No demo enables the origin. Builds clean: clang unity, GCC unity-off. Runs: plain `ran 706 of 12005 tests (phases: 3 5 17 18 28; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 45 s`, asan `ran 652 of 12005 tests (... flavors: asan ...) in 144 s`, no failures; tsan not run (no concurrency code). Reduced runs. `/code-review high`: 3 findings, all recorded. **Next: Day 5 (spatial, when you say).**

**Double precision, Day 5 done (2026-11-23, Linux): the spatial module in double precision.** `engine/spatial` (grids, static and dynamic BVH, pair tracker, queries, sync, debug draw) stores and queries `Aabbd`, `Sphered`, `Rayd`, `Frustumd` and `Planed`; `SpatialBounds::local` stays `f32` and `WorldBounds::box` is `Aabbd`, filled by the new `PlaceAabb(local, Placement)` (`math/placement.h`). Physics, audio, the renderer cull and two demos widen or narrow at the call (their own days convert them). Memory per box doubles (static node 56 bytes; test budgets raised). New test: the same scene 100,000 km away gives the same pairs and query results. Builds clean: clang unity, GCC unity-off. Runs: plain `ran 4488 of 12008 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 99 s`, asan `ran 3675 of 12008 tests (... asan ...) in 180 s`, tsan `ran 3306 of 12008 tests (... tsan ...) in 254 s`; only the known failures (minted DXIL and Metal libraries, voice-cost snapshot, LuaJIT sandbox flake). Reduced runs. `/code-review high`: 3 findings, all nits, recorded. **Next: Day 6 (physics bodies and broad phase, when you say).**

**Double precision, Day 6 done (2026-11-23, Linux): physics bodies, broad phase and queries.** Body positions (`Body::position`, `previous_position`, `BodyDesc::position`, `SetTransform`, `WorldCenter`, `Interpolated`, the joint helpers) are `Vec3d`, positions within `kMaxWorldCoordinate` (1e12 m); the integration, the broad phase's boxes (`Aabbd`), the spatial feed, `StateHash` (positions as `f64`), snapshot version 3 and input log version 2 follow. Queries take `Vec3d` origins and return `Vec3d` points (`PhysicsHit::point`; normals and distances `f32`) and put the other colliders in their own frame, so they are exact far away already; `Shape::Bounds(Vec3d, ...)` is new; the character controller walks in `f64`. The narrow phase and the solver still narrow positions to `f32` in world coordinates (Day 7), so the stack/pile/bridge test 10,000 km away moved there; new `tests/physics/far_world_test.cpp` covers free fall, pairs, queries, snapshot and limits. Hashes re-baselined (old and new in the day file): the golden table (equal for 1 to 32 threads), the step, joint and hinge constants, the C sample, the render-features demo and the script demo's cross-machine hash (Windows and macOS runs pending, KNOWN-ISSUES). `physics_bench` drop 5000: 17.8 to 18.9 ms falling, 32.0 to 34.0 ms landing (+6 percent). Builds clean: clang unity, GCC unity-off. Runs: plain `ran 901 of 12023 tests (phases: 3 23 25 27 28; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 68 s`, asan `ran 823 of 12023 tests (... asan ...) in 165 s`, tsan `ran 625 of 12023 tests (... tsan ...) in 242 s`, no failure. **Double precision, Day 7 done (2026-11-23, Linux): physics narrow phase, solver and joints.** Contacts are body-relative: `ContactManifold::anchor` (the first body's origin) is the point the manifold's points are measured from; `PoseFrom` places both shapes relative to an anchor (differences in `f64`, narrowed once) for the step, trigger tests, continuous collision and the queries; the solver keeps each body's start origin and offset, so arms, cached local points and the centre-of-mass motion are small numbers; joints measure their two sides from each other and a joint to the world from the new `JointDesc::world_origin`; `ContactEvent::point` is a `Vec3d`; snapshot version 4. Scenes 10,000 km away (ten stacked boxes, a pyramid, a plank bridge, a world pendulum, a bullet at a wall) match the origin within 4.2 micrometres after 600 steps (the new test fails on the previous commit); a character walks over a ledge there within 1 mm. Hashes moved again and were re-baselined (golden table equal for 1 to 32 threads; step, joint, hinge, C sample, render-features demo, script demo; old and new in the day file; Windows and macOS runs pending). `physics_bench` drop 5000: falling 18.8 ms, landing 33.4 ms (Day 1: 17.8 and 32.0); the per-pair subtraction is within noise, so no spec amendment. Builds clean: clang unity, GCC unity-off. Runs: plain `ran 902 of 12026 tests (phases: 3 23 25 27 28; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 62 s`, asan `ran 824 of 12026 tests (... asan ...) in 158 s`, tsan `ran 626 of 12026 tests (... tsan ...) in 224 s`, no failure. Reduced runs. `/code-review high`: 4 findings, all nits, recorded. **Next: Day 8 done, see below.**

**Phase 28 Day 492 done (2026-11-23, Linux): bodies and characters on the height field.** Contacts of every shape against the field's triangles, with a new rule that the field is solid below (a body that sank deep is brought out upward, not through; found by the 64 by 64 random-bump test), the debug drawer shows the cells under bodies (`DebugDrawOptions::field_cells`), and a terrain golden scene (100 bodies, hashes at 0, 1, 2, 8 and 32 threads). **Log everything (maintenance series, built 2026-11-20 on Linux; `plans/maintenance/log-everything.md`, 5 day files).** All program output goes through the log system: the prefix-free output channel (`out::Print`, `PrintText`, `PrintLine`, `PrintBytes`, `Flush`...; `log/output.h`) reaches every log device byte for byte, and `os::Print*` is removed (CLAUDE.md's wrappers table names `out::Print` and `Log`). ~690 calls migrated in `game/`, `tools/`, `tests/`, `engine/audio` and the docs' samples; `script_demo`'s diagnostics are now `Log` lines. Closing runs: plain `ran 4370 of 11706`, asan `ran 3572 of 11706`, tsan `ran 3222 of 11706` (partial; only known and no-GPU failures). Review recorded in Day 5. Not built on the PC or the Mac.

**Log devices (maintenance series, built 2026-11-20, Linux; `plans/maintenance/log-devices.md`, 5 days).** `LogDevice` (`log/log_device.h`) with a registry of 8 added devices and a console device chosen by `SetConsoleLogMode` (auto, plain, color, off): `StdoutLogDevice` (default), `TtyLogDevice` (coloured, control bytes shown as `^X`) on a colour-capable terminal (`os/terminal.h`: `NO_COLOR`, `CLICOLOR_FORCE`, `TERM`, `COLORTERM`; Windows enables VT), `io::FileLogDevice` (buffer, flush at Error, size rotation) and a test `CaptureLogDevice`. Settings `log.console` and `log.file.{path,level,max_bytes,keep}`; the C API callback stays an observer. Crash and assert output still writes straight to the sink. Not built on the PC or the Mac (see `KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). `CURRENT_PHASE` stays 27.

**The engine is named Visual Storm Engine (VSE) (2026-11-20, Linux, the user's decision).** The C++ namespace `engine_placeholder` is now `vse` (lowercase; 5,020 uses in 1,938 files, including the generated tables and the committed shader outputs), the CMake project is `vse`, and the README, `PLAN.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and the hello_window title say Visual Storm Engine. Kept as they were, by the user's choice: the `ENGINE_*` macros, the `engine_c` C API (`engine_c.h`, the ABI snapshot) and `libengine`. Formatted with `scripts/format.sh`, built clean (unity, plain and asan flavors in `build`), `docs` regenerated, `ctest -L docs` passes. Scoped run: `scripts/test-phase.sh --affected` ran everything of the plain flavor (the root `CMakeLists.txt` changed); what failed is in `KNOWN-ISSUES.md` (all seen before the rename). No end-of-day review: a mechanical rename. Not built on the Windows PC or the Mac.

**Container audit fixes (maintenance series, planned 2026-11-19; `plans/maintenance/container-audit.md`, 12 day files under `plans/maintenance/container-audit/`).**
- The audit (`plans/maintenance/container-audit-2026-11-19.md`, read-only, nothing timed) found linear scans and quadratic paths across the engine, most chosen because the
  foundation lacked a container feature. It also found three correctness problems: `DeviceResources`' 256-owner cap, hashed path keys never compared, and an unsorted
  "sorted" joint list.
- The Mac's `eefa8c3b` (move-only `HashMap` entries, heterogeneous lookup, `ForEach`, `Hash<StringView>`/`Hash<String>`) is in. On Linux, `foundation_base_tests`
  builds with Clang and passes 132 cases; its other builds and docs are Day 1's.
- The series (the user's choice of maintenance series over a phase, with benchmarks first):
  - Day 1: baselines.
  - Days 2-3: the foundation (map, set, byte hash, deque, heap, vector, string, sorts).
  - Day 4: one FNV and string keys.
  - Days 5-6: the renderer and the UI.
  - Day 7: `DenseMap` and real key types (inserted 2026-11-19 at the user's request).
  - Days 8-11: the modules.
  - Day 12: Windows and macOS (the user's).
  - Day 13: a gate, the after numbers, the full closing runs and an uncapped review.
- **Day 1 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - `game/container_bench` (the containers alone, and the audited engine paths) and the baselines of every audited path; the table is in the day file.
    Quadratic: `SetParent`, `Value` maps, `MemoryStore` puts and the character queries. Rays run at 8,700/s against 5,000 bodies. `DeviceResources` takes 256 owners.
    Linear after all: script waits and events, `ResourceTable` removal.
  - The Mac's `HashMap` checked on Linux: `<X11/X.h>`'s `DestroyAll` macro broke a GCC non-unity build. Fixed with the X11 guard (`containers/hash_map.h`
    included before the X11 region of the two X11 tests, `DestroyAll` in `x11_enable.h`/`x11_disable.h`). `docs/reference` regenerated.
  - Runs: plain `ran 4077 of 11341 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: excluded)`, three known failures; asan and tsan in the day file.
  - Review: 5 findings, all fixed.
- **Day 2 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - `containers/hash.h` (`Fnv1a64`, `Fnv1a64Builder`, `HashCombine`, the `Hash<K>` specializations); `HashMap` completed (lazy, power of two,
    `Clear`/`Reserve`/`Contains`/`Emplace`, range-for); `HashSet`; `Hash<ContentHash>`; `String(StringView)`.
  - Fixed: keys of another type are converted before hashing.
  - Tests deferred to Day 13 at the user's request (the series now builds and reviews per day and tests once at the end); the container tests pass.
  - Review: 3 findings, all fixed.
- **Day 3 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - `Vector` swap-remove, append, emplace, clone, swap and range-for; bulk `String` append, reserve and clear; `Deque`; `BinaryHeap`.
  - Search (`LowerBound`/`UpperBound`/`BinarySearch`), selection (`NthElement`/`PartialSort`), `StableSort` for any movable type, and sorts of containers.
  - Tests deferred to Day 13; the container tests pass. Review: 3 findings, all fixed.
- **Day 4 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - One FNV-1a: 22 files call `Fnv1a64`, with values kept (`kFnv1a64LegacyBasis` for the truncated basis nine copies used).
  - Hash-only keys fixed: `asset_mint` paths, the LuaJIT profiler, `script_reload`.
  - `ResourceTable` keyed by `String` with O(1) `Remove`: 1.29 ms down to 378 ns per removal at 10,000 paths (Day 1's flat number was an invalid bench, corrected).
  - Five `HashSet`s; `Clear()` instead of re-made maps.
  - Tests deferred to Day 13. Review: 2 findings, both fixed.
- **Day 5 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - `DeviceResources` takes any number of owners (O(1) registration, one sort per reset step), tested with 10,000.
  - The material pipeline cache and the theme cache are hashed; the theme cache replaces entries instead of emptying itself.
  - Variant names are mapped; the overlay filter, callback snapshots and text field edits are cheaper.
  - Tests deferred to Day 13. Review: 1 finding, fixed.
- **Day 6 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - The G-buffer and light sorts are typed (the lights by `PartialSort`); the draw list sorts with a kept scratch.
  - Unchanged scene labels are not laid out again; `SetPixelSize` returns early; the Vulkan free list frees by binary search.
  - Bind-group caching and some small allocations deferred (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Tests deferred to Day 13. Review: no findings.
- **Day 7 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - `DenseMap` (entries dense in slots, a slot index, the key stored once).
  - The theme cache and `ResourceTable` use it; `DeviceResources` and the label cache are plain `HashMap`s.
  - The pipeline and label caches use real key types (`PipelineKey`, `LabelKey`/`LabelKeyView`), so nothing is keyed by a bare hash.
  - `HashSet` and `HashMap` share `detail::OpenTable` (`containers/open_table.h`), and the set stores bare keys (the user's request).
  - Tests deferred to Day 13. Review: 1 finding, fixed.
- **Day 8 done in part (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - The joint pairs are a set (the "sorted keys" comment fixed); the solver and pair tracker swap instead of copying; the hash grid's inner search is gone.
  - The query, wake and destroy indexes are deferred with reasons (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).
  - Physics determinism, hash and joint tests and the spatial suite pass; the rest waits for Day 13. Review: no findings.
- **Day 9 done (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - `Value` maps are indexed past 8 keys (8,000 keys: 4,984 to 123 ns per op); the command overlay, suspended coroutines and task keys are `HashMap`s.
  - Query pages are selected, not fully sorted; the debugger queue is a `Deque`.
  - The script suites pass (240 + 5 + 35 + 19 cases); the rest waits for Day 13. Review: no findings.
- **Day 10 done in part (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - The C API surface scene's entries, lights, meshes and materials are `DenseMap`s (O(1) per drawn entity, same removal order).
  - `MemoryStore` is a `HashMap` with sorted keys for scans (10,000 random puts: 2,975 to 145 ns each); the UDP and simulated reorder buffers are keyed by sequence.
  - The ECS hierarchy index is deferred (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Review: no findings.
- **Day 11 done in part (2026-11-19, Linux):**
  - Settings documents are indexed by key (2,000 keys: 3,088 to 314 ns each); the async load queue is a `BinaryHeap`.
  - `asset_mint` level names are indexed; long combining-mark runs are counting-sorted.
  - The resource manager, blob cache, backends and others are deferred (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Review: no findings.
- **Day 12, the Windows PC's half done (2026-11-19):**
  - D3D12: per-list resource-state indexes (a list touching 2,000 buffers: 5.26 to 4.19 ms, mostly validation), a hashed sampler-table cache
    with a free list, the pipeline keys through `LowerBound` and `Fnv1a64Builder` (values unchanged). The `select` poller is indexed by handle.
  - Found and fixed in the series' shared code: `HashMap`/`HashSet` growth moved the key before hashing it on MSVC (271 segfaults),
    `String` appends grew twice (an arena ran out); the gate violations were fixed on Linux by Day 13 the same way.
  - Runs after the rebase onto Day 13 (ctest, the reset loop and crash tests left out): `build-msvc` plain 4022 of 4024, asan 3442 of 3443,
    `build-clang-cl` plain 4050 of 4054; only known failures (voice cost, the jobs flake, the `engine_c_runtime` hang) and the dx12 entry
    of `container_use_check`'s allow list, now stale and removed. The physics and script hashes are unchanged. Review: 5 findings, 1 fixed,
    3 recorded, 1 no change needed.
- **Day 12, the Mac's half done (2026-11-19):**
  - Metal: a stage's slot refs are sorted once by (set, binding) and `RefFor` binary-searches them (not the planned per-encoder map:
    nothing is rebuilt per encoder); the layout sort and `CopySlots` use the container overloads; `MetalArchiveFnv` is `Fnv1a64Builder`,
    its values pinned in a test, and `container_use_check`'s Metal entry is gone.

    bound and the three issues above, fixed after; arm64 compile-only clean; docs 7 of 7. Hashes equal Linux's and the PC's (physics
    golden, `script_sample`, the 150-frame demo hash, the 600-frame demo `424a6fbf279d2104`); `script_demo_window_cocoa` passes. Also: the

    shown failing), 1 moot, 1 recorded, 1 declined.

    were fixed; the remaining failures are known from Phase 27.

  - The before/after table is in the day file. Series review: 7 findings (3 fixed, 3 left, 1 no change).
- **Sort follow-up (2026-11-19, the user's request):**
  - `Sort` is a pattern-defeating quicksort: 5 to 20 times faster on sorted, reversed and all-equal input, 2 times on few distinct keys, and 22% on
    random 56-byte elements; about 7% slower on random `u32`.
  - The G-buffer keeps `qsort` (measured). Full suite: `ran 11249 of 11379`, only the known failures.
  - Then the G-buffer's draw sort moved off `qsort` (not portable): a `StableSort` of 24-byte proxies, 0.99 ms per frame against `qsort`'s 1.02 at
    10,000 objects. Renderer tests run for it only (see the day file).
- **Maintenance series [the query index and the hierarchy index](plans/maintenance/query-and-hierarchy-indices.md) (2026-11-19, the user's request):**
  - **Day 1 done:** physics queries go through a query index kept current by the world, with results bit-identical to the full scan, which the
    oracle tests compare. Rays against 5,000 bodies run 98 times faster (8,637 to 846,000 per second), and 2,000 characters step in 10 ms
    (294 before). The step pays about 0.55 ms at 5,000 falling bodies.
  - Runs: plain `ran 4236 of 11391 tests`, asan `ran 3494 of 11391`, tsan `ran 3149 of 11391` (phases: all; display included; crash tests
    excluded), with only known failures and flakes. Review: 1 fixed, 2 declined.
  - **Day 2 done, series closed:**
    - `SetParent`, `ClearParent` and `DestroyRecursive` keep a children index beside the hierarchy. It is checked against the world's new
      `ComponentVersion<Parent>()` and rebuilt once after an outside change.
    - `SetParent` costs 113-122 ns at 1,000 and 4,000 entities, against 4.9 and 19.2 µs.
    - An oracle of the old code agrees on statuses, depths, storage order and destroy order over random histories.
    - Closing run: `ran 10888 of 11400 tests (phases: all; flavors: all; display: included; crash tests: excluded)`, known failures only.
    - Series review: 4 findings (1 fixed, 2 declined, 1 no change). Windows and macOS wait.
- `CURRENT_PHASE` stays 27, and the next phase follows the series.

**Shorter test runs: Days 1-4 of 5 done on Linux, Day 5's Mac half done (2026-11-19)** ([plans/maintenance/test-time.md](plans/maintenance/test-time.md); the user confirmed the decisions).
- **Day 1:** `scripts/test-phase.sh` builds only the chosen flavors' targets (`engine_tests_plain`, `_asan`, `_tsan`), and in
  parallel. After touching `gbuffer.h` that is 21 s for plain, against 357 s before. `test_phase_script_flavor_targets` checks that every
  test's programs are built by its flavor's target. Ninja and sccache are measured: Ninja rebuilds about 40% faster, and switching the
  Linux tree is the user's call.
- **Day 2:** a day starts with `scripts/test-phase.sh --start-day`. `--affected` compares with that commit (else the upstream merge-base,
  else the phase's start) and prints the base. The unlit change now selects 1,651 tests plain, against every phase before.
  - Run: `ran 562 of 11462 tests (phases: 3 6 27; flavors: plain; ...) in 164 s`, no failures.
- **Day 3:** the three `*_large` runs carry the `soak` label. Scoped runs leave them out, and `--all` and `--soak` keep them. After a run
  the script warns about any unlabelled test over 30 s (60 s for a sanitizer test).

  - The warning lists 13 tests, Day 4's work.
- **Day 4:** measured alone, most GPU tests on the warning's list take 1-4 s. They were long only while sharing the GPU, so the day was
  re-planned.
  - Short forms (`tests/support/soak.h`): the reset loop runs 20 resets, 100 in soak; the hostile scripts run ten seconds, a minute in soak.
  - Gates: `test_phase_labels` 43 s to 3.2 s, `platform_neutral_check` 28.5 s to 6.1 s, `file_access_check` 17.3 s to 8.75 s.
  - The plain suite's wall time is now the window tests' serial `display` chain (115-205 s); that is the next lever.

  `script_demo_window_*_long`, are soak tests. The scripted game's window run is 120 frames. The window layer and the RHI presentation
  tests keep both backends. The saving is not measured yet: the desktop was in use.

  `engine_tests_plain`. It is now one deferred call in `cmake/EngineFlavorTargets.cmake`, not yet configured on the PC. The script and its ctests pass under

    `ran 4163 of 11291 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: included; soak: excluded) in 712 s`, with only the
    known voice-cost snapshot failing.

**Test binaries per platform (2026-11-19, Linux):** reading the CMake files found that the PC and the Mac never ran `animation_tests`, the Mac never ran `docs_script_api`, and `input_probe` on Windows registered Wayland and X11 runs instead of Win32. All three are registered now; not yet configured or run on the PC or the Mac (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).

**Unlit materials (2026-11-19, Linux)** ([plans/maintenance/unlit-materials.md](plans/maintenance/unlit-materials.md)). The glTF importer accepts `KHR_materials_unlit` (it refused it) and sets `renderer::kMaterialFlagUnlit` in the material's `flags`; the lighting and point-light passes show such a pixel as its base colour. Found by the user's `.glb` in the model viewer, which now opens and looks right. SPIR-V re-minted here; DXIL and Metal wait for the PC and the Mac.

**Model viewer done (2026-11-19, Linux)** ([plans/maintenance/model-viewer.md](plans/maintenance/model-viewer.md)). `model_viewer FILE.glb|FILE.gltf` imports the file in memory, spawns its default scene and frames it in a window; left-drag orbits, the wheel zooms, right/middle drag and W/A/S/D Q/E strafe, F reframes; a skinned model plays its clips (Space pauses, N next). `--screenshot` draws offscreen. Tests: the orbit camera (6 cases) and 7 ctests (screenshots of the robot, the meadow and a `.gltf`, bad files, windows on Wayland and X11). Runs: `--affected --flavor all` `ran 10923 of 11450 tests (phases: all; flavors: all; display: included; crash tests: excluded)`, known failures only. Review: 2 findings, fixed. Not done: the PC and the Mac; you trying it by hand.

**Crash handler chaining for the host, Day 1 done (2026-11-19, Linux)** ([plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining.md](plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining.md)). A signal handler a host installed before `crash_handler_install` now runs at a crash, after the engine's report and dump, once, on the faulting thread, and the original fault still ends the process. The host's own repro writes both its log marker and the engine's envelope. Runs: `--affected --flavor all --crashes` `ran 11437 of 11437 tests (phases: all; flavors: all; display: included; crash tests: included)`, known failures only. Review: 1 finding, fixed. **Next:** Day 2 (Windows, the PC) and Day 3 (macOS, the Mac).

The DXIL waits for the PC (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).

**Unlit materials, the PC's half (2026-11-19):** the DXIL of `lighting.frag` and `point_light.frag` was re-minted with the pinned slangc and dxcompiler; no other `.dxil.inc` changed. On the PC these pass: the DXIL staleness test, `shaders_check`, `shaders_portability` and the unlit GPU test on Vulkan and Direct3D 12 (15 of 15 shader and unlit tests in MSVC, plain and asan; 9 of 9 in clang-cl). The renderer phases 4, 5, 8, 13 and 24 pass too, 742 of 742 (plain, MSVC, the reset loop left out).

**Planned (2026-11-19, on the PC): glTF coverage** ([plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage.md)). This imports and draws what a typical exported model loses today: the metallic-roughness, occlusion and emissive textures (as slots with default textures; emissive gets its own HDR G-buffer target), `COLOR_0` (`MESH` v2), morph targets with weight animation (`MESH` v3, a `MorphWeights` component, `ANIM` v2, the `MORPHED` vertex option), and `KHR_lights_punctual` (a new spot light type, `LightSource` v2). There are fifteen days: four strands on Linux, then DXIL on the PC, Metal on the Mac, and a closing day. The user confirmed the eight decisions as written (2026-11-19) and started the series on the PC.

**glTF coverage Day 1 done (2026-11-19, the Windows PC; [01-metal-rough-occlusion-maps.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/01-metal-rough-occlusion-maps.md)):** every G-buffer template has linear `metallic_roughness_tex` and `occlusion_tex` slots (white by default) and an `occlusion_strength` parameter, with glTF's math in `gbuffer.frag`. The shader's SPIR-V and DXIL are minted; its Metal library waits for Day 14. A GPU equivalence test (Vulkan and Direct3D 12) passes, and fails without the shader change. A new `renderer_bench --section gpu` shows no measurable cost. Review: 4 findings, 2 fixed (the C API's new slot names had no test, and its error message lacked them). Runs (ctest, scoped phases plus asan): MSVC plain 1737 of 1737 and asan 3459 of 3460 (the expected Metal staleness test). Clang-cl 1734 of 1737: three C API tests failed under load and passed alone. Shared trees 89 of 89 each.

**glTF coverage Day 2 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [02-emissive-target.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/02-emissive-target.md)), tests skipped at the user's request:** emission is an HDR colour in a fourth G-buffer target (`RGBA16_Float`), `emissive_color` times `emissive_tex`, added once by the lighting pass. The C API's `emissive_scale` became `emissive_color` and `emissive_tex`. This also fixes a Day 1 bug: `occlusion_strength` defaulted to 0. Built in `build-msvc` only; the tests ran partly (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).

**glTF coverage Day 3 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [03-import-pbr-maps.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/03-import-pbr-maps.md)), tests not run:** the importer binds glTF's metallic-roughness, occlusion (with its strength) and emissive textures. An ORM texture is made once and bound to both slots. The manifest keys of existing textures are unchanged.

**glTF coverage Day 4 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [04-mesh-colour-stream.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/04-mesh-colour-stream.md)), tests not run:** a mesh blob can carry per-vertex colours (`UNorm8x4` at location 4, sRGB). No version bump was needed, since version 1 already accepts the attribute. `.mesh.src` takes a colour.

**glTF coverage Day 5 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [05-import-vertex-colours.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/05-import-vertex-colours.md)), tests not run:** `COLOR_0` becomes the mesh's colour stream. A material shared by coloured and uncoloured meshes gets a `<name>_color` twin on the colour template, which the level's coloured slots name.

**glTF coverage Day 6 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [06-mesh-morph-section.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/06-mesh-morph-section.md)), tests not run:** a mesh blob holds up to 64 morph targets (sections 40 and 41), read in place and checked on open. Its box covers weights from -1 to 1. Version 1 already refused section 40, so there was no version bump.

**glTF coverage Day 7 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [07-gpu-morph-targets.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/07-gpu-morph-targets.md)), tests not run or written:** a compute pre-pass (`shaders/morph.comp`) applies up to 8 targets per object into a frame buffer, and the G-buffer pass draws from it, skinning after the morph. This replaced the vertex-shader plan, which hit the four-bind-group limit. `GpuMesh` keeps the deltas; `RenderScene::SetMorphWeights` sets them.

**glTF coverage Day 8 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [08-morph-weights-component.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/08-morph-weights-component.md)), tests not run:** `scene::MorphWeights` is a stored component (`morph_weights`). The render sync hands it to the render objects, and the base shape returns when it goes. (Day 9 moved it to `animation::MorphWeights`.)

**glTF coverage Day 9 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [09-clip-weight-tracks.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/09-clip-weight-tracks.md)), tests not run:** a clip can carry a weight track (section 20, raw `f32` keys) that animates the playing entity's `MorphWeights`, with or without a skeleton. The animation system samples it.

**glTF coverage Day 10 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [10-import-morph-targets.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/10-import-morph-targets.md)), tests not run:** the importer brings in morph targets (no longer refused), the default weights (as a level piece) and `weights` animation channels (into the skin's clip, or a weights-only clip; cubic splines approximated). This also fixes a Day 7 `docgen` error.

**glTF coverage Day 11 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [11-spot-lights.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/11-spot-lights.md)), tests not run:** spot lights (`LightType::Spot`, glTF's cone falloff) drawn through the point-light pass, with `LightData` still 48 bytes. `scene::LightSource` version 2 adds the cone angles, with an upgrade from version 1.

**glTF coverage Day 12 done (2026-11-19, the PC; [12-import-punctual-lights.md](plans/maintenance/gltf-coverage/12-import-punctual-lights.md)), tests not run:** the importer brings in `KHR_lights_punctual`: each node's light becomes a `light_source` on its entity, in the engine's units (the `assets.import.light_*` settings). The model viewer uses a file's own lights. Every level loader registers the scene components. Next: the whole suite, once, on the PC; then Day 13.

**Skinned model fixes (2026-11-20, Linux; [plans/maintenance/skinned-model-fixes.md](plans/maintenance/skinned-model-fixes.md)):** the user's character `.glb` now opens in the model viewer. Fixed: the clip loader refused a clip of morph weights only; the animation system skipped such a clip on a skinned mesh; `SceneFrame` never read `renderer.joint_palette_bytes` (1024 matrices); the importer put a skin in the scene's space, so a scaled root counted twice (`kGltfImporterOutputVersion` 3); the viewer gives each clip to the entities that can play it, draws unposed skins in their bind pose, and frames every mesh as drawn (skinned ones by their joints' boxes under the palette; morphed ones by their vertices' own box), so the whole character is in the picture; the renderer culls a skinned object by its pose (the blob's joint boxes under the palette, grown by its morph targets), refreshed by `RenderSync` each frame (`ran 1669 of 11530 tests (...; plain ...)`, asan and tsan of the same selection clean; review 4 findings, 2 fixed, 2 kept). Runs: `ran 1505 of 11521 tests (phases: 3 4 5 22 23 27; flavors: plain; ...) in 51 s`, only the known `engine_c_surface`; docs 9 of 9; asan and tsan of the changed suites 70 of 70. **C API script physics done on Linux (2026-11-20; [plans/maintenance/c-api-script-physics.md](plans/maintenance/c-api-script-physics.md), design [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-20-c-api-script-physics-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-20-c-api-script-physics-design.md), one day at the user's request):** a C API world's Lua behaviors have `physics.*` and `character.*`, on that world's physics, with the layer names from `script.physics_layers` and the step's contact and trigger events. The world keeps one `physics::CharacterSet` (moved from `script_physics` to `physics`) for both doors: `ENGINE_COMPONENT_CHARACTER`, `character_move` (now a held move carried out in each fixed step; `dt` unused) and `character_jump` drive the same characters the scripts see, and the component follows the set. The write-back is interpolated; the C++ layer's `MoveCharacter`, `JumpCharacter` and `PhysicsSnapshot` say so (a follow-up commit). New: `character.set_position` (a teleport), `character.create` on an entity spawned in the same tick, and physics snapshots that hold the characters' state. Found on the way and fixed: `PhysicsWorld::Restore` dropped the pre-step hook, so restored characters stopped. Test: `host_script_character.c` (an imported ground; the apex, landing, agreement with the C API, the teleport; 1.4750 m walked at 30, 60 and 120 fps). Runs: plain `ran 4321 of 11634 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded; soak: excluded) in 116 s` (docs_check and docs_script_api failed before the last docs regeneration and pass after it; the known Debug audio bound; an X11 clipboard test failed once under load and passed alone), asan `ran 3562 of 11634` and tsan `ran 3212 of 11634` clean, `ctest -L docs` 9 of 9. The script comments that said Linux only are corrected. **Crash handler chaining, Day 3 done (2026-11-19, the Mac)** ([plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining/03-macos.md](plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining/03-macos.md)). On macOS a host's handler installed before `crash_handler_install` now runs once after the report, the dump and the envelope, on the faulting thread with the kernel's `siginfo`, for a fault and for an abort; the process then dies of the original signal (without such a handler nothing changes: Breakpad's `_exit`). Breakpad ended the process right after the dump, so it has a local change (`third_party/sentry_native/VENDORED.md`, the user's decision): a switch the engine sets only when the host has a handler for the crash's signal makes it resume its suspended threads and forward the exception itself (libSystem's `exc_server` cannot find its handler in the shared library), and the engine relays the signal once. Tests: seven macOS death tests (five shown failing without the change, the faulting-`SIGABRT` one without its review fix) and `crash_host.c` on macOS (shared tree). Runs (plain, display excluded: the console was locked): `ran 4139 of 11254 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: included)`, only the known voice-cost failure; after the review, macOS crash tests 36 of 36, phase 7 159 of 159; arm64 compile-only clean; docs 7 of 7. Review: 10 findings, 8 fixed, 1 recorded, 1 kept as designed. Next: Day 2 (Windows, the PC).

**Crash handler chaining for the host, Day 1 done (2026-11-19, Linux)** ([plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining.md](plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining.md)). A signal handler a host installed before `crash_handler_install` now runs at a crash, after the engine's report and dump, once, on the faulting thread, and the original fault still ends the process. The host's own repro writes both its log marker and the engine's envelope. Runs: `--affected --flavor all --crashes` `ran 11437 of 11437 tests (phases: all; flavors: all; display: included; crash tests: included)`, known failures only. Review: 1 finding, fixed. **Next:** Day 2 (Windows, the PC); Day 3 (macOS) is done, below.

**Crash handler chaining Day 2 done (2026-11-19, the Windows PC; [02-windows.md](plans/maintenance/host-crash-handler-chaining/02-windows.md)).** On Windows, Breakpad called the filter it replaced only when its dump failed. Now the crash gate (`os/windows/debug/crash_hook.cpp`) calls the host's earlier unhandled-exception filter once its dump is written: once, on the faulting thread, with the original exception, the gate back on top first. The process still ends with the original code. An `abort()` reaches the host's earlier `SIGABRT` handler (the library already called it) and no filter, as without the engine. There are 6 new death tests and `crash_host.c`'s marker, and the four filter tests fail without the change. Guides: `foundation-debugging.md`, `engine-library.md` (with `crash.dump_directory` given at creation), and `BACKEND.md`. Review: 2 findings, both fixed (a second thread faulting during the host's filter was dumped twice; regression test shown failing). Runs, by ctest directly with crash tests included. Before the rebase onto Day 1: `build-msvc` plain 4179 of 4183 passed (the known voice-cost snapshot and `Throughput`, plus the mouse test that reads the real cursor and a once-only failure of a new test, both filed as flaky), and `build-clang-cl` plain 4212 of 4213 (voice-cost). After it: `build-msvc` plain 4192 of 4195 (voice-cost, `Throughput`, and a once-only asset async-load failure, 5 of 5 alone), asan 3594 of 3596, docs 8 of 8, `build-clang-cl` 243 of 243 of phases 7 and 23 and the crash tests, and 89 of 89 `engine_library` tests in each shared tree. The asan runs did not leave out the phase 8 reset loop by mistake (it passed before the rebase and failed after it; the PC did not freeze). The other asan failure was the abort-flags crash test, once, 5 of 5 alone. A crash child hung under load in two loaded crash-only runs (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). All six trees build clean. After the rebase onto Day 3, all six trees build clean, the crash, phase 7, phase 23 and docs tests pass (411 of 411 in MSVC, 260 of 260 in clang-cl), and so do the 89 `engine_library` tests in each shared tree. With Days 1 (Linux) and 3 (the Mac), the series is done on all three machines.

**Phase 27 Day 474 done (2026-11-19, the Mac): LuaJIT, the FSEvents watcher and the script tests on macOS.** LuaJIT builds from CMake on macOS (host tools for the host processor, `buildvm -m machasm`, x64 or arm64 by `CMAKE_OSX_ARCHITECTURES`); the script modules, tests, `script_demo` and `script_bench` are in the macOS build. `io::DirectoryWatcher` has a native macOS backend, FSEvents (`io/macos/fsevents_watcher.cpp`; chosen over kqueue by the spike), deciding by `lstat` against a known-name map; a rename is two events. **Live spatial voices (maintenance, 2026-11-18, Linux; `plans/maintenance/host-live-spatial-voices.md`).** C API: push streams (`audio_stream_open_push`, `audio_stream_push`, `audio_stream_push_i16`, `audio_stream_stats`), a `stream` field on `engine_audio_source` so an entity's spatial voice plays a live stream, `ENGINE_CAP_AUDIO_PUSH_STREAMS`; `StreamBuffer::CreatePush` is a lock-free ring read by the mixer voice (oldest frames dropped and counted, optional prefill, never ends, closed by `audio_stream_close`). A live tone equals the clip's at five places; delay at most 256 frames (5.33 ms) on Linux. Windows and Mac figures are theirs (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).

**Slot UV transform (maintenance, 2026-11-18, Linux; `plans/maintenance/host-slot-uv-transform.md`).** Material slots `albedo_uv` (`ENGINE_SLOT_VEC4`: scale, offset; default (1,1,0,0)) and `albedo_uv_outside` (linear RGBA; alpha above 0 masks the part outside the picture and keeps the sampled coordinate half a texel inside the edge) on the G-buffer material, settable per mesh slot; `gbuffer.frag` re-minted (SPIR-V here; the DXIL and Metal outputs and the fitting cases on D3D12 and Metal are the PC's and the Mac's, `KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). `host_acceptance_scene` fits 4:3 and 9:16 pictures into the 16:9 screen.

**The host resource-cache API (maintenance, 2026-11-18, Linux; `plans/maintenance/host-resource-cache-api.md`).** C API: `resource_read`, `resource_add_many` (atomic, `HostMemoryBackend::AddMany`) with an asynchronous form, `import_format_version`, `ENGINE_CAP_RESOURCE_CACHE`, and `engine_import_result.summary` (nodes, bounds, materials, animations as JSON); C++ wrapper, ABI snapshot and guides updated. **Phase 27 Day 469 done (2026-11-19, Linux): persistence, snapshots and replay.** `persist(...)` marks the fields of `self` a save keeps; `ScriptRuntime::CapturePersistent` writes them (entity handles as stable keys) into `script_persist` components and `RegisterScriptComponents` registers `script` and `script_persist` with the storage module (custom, bounded codecs over the new `Value` record, `value_codec.*`); after a load `ResolveLoaded` and `RestoreClock` give the fields back to new instances before `on_load` and `on_start`; `script_media::ReplayLog` records input snapshots with per-tick hashes. 10,000 scripted entities save and load; a session saved at tick 3 and continued at 1, 2, 3, 7 and 16 threads ends on the control's hashes; a session recorded at 16 threads replays at 1, 2, 3, 7 and 16, and the reverse. Review: 7 findings, 2 fixed, 5 declined or recorded. Runs: phase 27 plain `ran 291 of 11282 tests (phases: 27; flavors: plain; display: excluded; crash tests: excluded)`, asan `ran 284 of 11282`, tsan `ran 284 of 11282`; reduced runs. Coroutines, timers, queued events and task results are not saved (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Next: Day 470.

**Phase 27 Day 465 done (2026-11-18, Linux): debugging.** Script errors are reported once per distinct error per instance (`behavior 'x' on entity K ('Name'): on_update failed: script:line: message` plus a trace line; repeats counted, shown at powers of ten), as an `on_script_error` event and in `RecentErrors()` and `ScriptRuntimeStats::distinct_errors`. The backend interface has a debug session (line events, stack and locals, `State::SetDebugSession`), a profiler and a console `Eval`; LuaJIT implements them over one hook dispatcher. `ScriptDebugger` + `DebugTransport` (in-process transport): breakpoints, step over/in/out, pause, stack, locals, detach; `script.debug_pause_all` (default true) holds every other partition at a gate between callbacks. `ConsoleRun`/`ConsoleSubmit`/`ConsoleTake`, `script.profile` and `ProfileReport`. **Phase 27 Day 464 done (2026-11-18, Linux): hot reload.** `script::ScriptReloader` compiles a changed script in a scratch state (a broken edit is reported and the old version keeps running), then `ScriptWorld::ReloadModules` replaces it, and every script that requires it, in every partition between ticks in two phases (run, then commit with `on_reload_save`/`on_reload_restore`), all or nothing; once per script per tick; off unless `script.hot_reload`. New file-layer facility `io::DirectoryWatcher` (inotify on Linux, a polling backend elsewhere until the Windows and macOS backends on Days 473-474, merged events, `Overflow`), used by `ScriptDevWatcher`; `asset_mint mint-script --dev-out`. **Phase 27 Day 463 done (2026-11-18, Linux): minted script assets II: loading through the resource manager.** Scripts load through the resource manager (`ScriptView` loader, `script::ScriptAssets`): the dependency closure loads whole or fails by value, each script is added once after the scripts it requires, and the world compiles each from the shared buffer range without running it (`State::DefineModule`, no text spliced) and runs it once per partition; `__require(key)` works only while a script loads and only for declared dependencies; `script.dev_directory` is declared. `/code-review high`: 4 findings (2 fixed, 2 declined), with regression tests. Runs: phase 27 plain 198, asan 191, tsan 191 of 10982; phase 22 plain 451 (reduced runs); gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 464.

**Phase 27 Day 462 done (2026-11-18, Linux): minted script assets I: the SCRP format and the minter.** `SCRP` blobs (`asset/script_blob.h`: source, language, entry points, id-based dependencies, optional bytecode behind a key; read in place by `ScriptView`) and `asset_mint mint-script`, which normalizes and validates a script (banned libraries, literal `require` names rewritten to ids, dependency graph with missing and cycle chains, syntax through the new `State::Compile`, behaviors and lifecycle callbacks listed by a run in a scratch state, optional `--bytecode`); minting is deterministic. `/code-review high`: 4 findings (2 fixed, 2 declined), with regression tests. Runs: phase 27 plain 189, asan 182, tsan 182 of 10955; phase 22 plain 451 (reduced runs); gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 463.

**Phase 27 Day 461 done (2026-11-18, Linux): script tasks: parallel work from Lua.** `task.run`, `task.map`, `task.parallel_for`, `task.result` and `wait_task` run source-text tasks on a pool of pure-binding states (`script::TaskPool`), at the end of the tick that asked, with results identical for any thread count or state count; settings `script.task_states`, `script.task_queue_limit`, `script.task_arg_bytes`; a million-index `parallel_for` runs in about 4.7 ms on 1 thread and 1.3 ms on 8. `/code-review high`: 3 findings (2 fixed, 1 declined) plus 4 security-review fixes, with regression tests. Runs: phase 27 plain 179, asan 172, tsan 172 of 10925 (reduced runs); gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 462.

**Phase 27 Day 460 done (2026-11-18, Linux): latent waits.** `wait`, `wait_frames` and `wait_event` yield a callback's coroutine and resume it on the sim clock or tick count; `spawn`, `stop`, `after`, `every` and `tween` are library code over them; `script.coroutine_limit` caps waiting coroutines per partition; event payload, subscription and chain resource limits from the Day 459 security review. `/code-review high`: 3 findings, all fixed (event-waiter scan, two `__spawn` forgery variants) with a regression test. Runs: phase 27 plain 164, asan 157, tsan 157 of 10865 (reduced runs); gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 461.

**Phase 27 Day 459 done (2026-11-18, Linux): events and callbacks.** `EventBus` (native emit and subscribe, chain limit), `event.emit`/`emit_to` bindings, `event.on`/`once`/`off` in the behavior host, delivery one tick after the emit in thread-independent order, `script.event_chain_limit`; about 5.6 million handler calls per second on 8 threads. `/code-review high`: 3 findings: 1 fixed (stale subscription from a closing instance), 2 declined (emit_to a placeholder, emit-cap test) with reasons in the day file. Runs: phase 27 plain 155, asan 148, tsan 148 of 10838 (reduced runs); gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 460.

**Phase 27 Day 458 done (2026-11-18, Linux): the parallel script system on the job scheduler.** `ScriptRuntime::Tick(world, dt, scheduler)` runs one job per partition (`ParallelForBlocking`); `MakeScriptSystem` passes the new `SystemContext::JobScheduler()`; per-partition stats and log buffers are merged in partition order after the jobs; 20,000 scripted entities give the same world hash at 1, 2, 3, 7 and 16 threads; the partition hash is even; scaling 119k to 904k entity updates per second from 1 to 16 threads. `/code-review high`: 2 findings: 1 fixed (log order depended on thread timing; per-partition log buffers), 1 declined (no log-capture hook to assert the order). Runs: phase 27 plain 147, asan 140, tsan 140 of 10814 (reduced runs); phases 17 and 19 plain; gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 459.

**Phase 27 Day 457 done (2026-11-18, Linux): script component and serial script system.** `ScriptComponent`, the Lua behavior host, `ScriptRuntime` (a world resource) and `MakeScriptSystem()` run create, enable, start, fixed, update, the merge and a second destroy phase; `script.on_error` (disable, retry, abort_in_debug); ECS gains `ReadsAllComponents`, `WritesAllComponents` and `ExclusiveWorld`. `/code-review high`: 5 findings: 3 fixed (on_disable on a dead entity, per-tick rebuild, missing load-failure test), 2 declined (f32 dt, reset/abort tests) with reasons in the day file. Runs: phase 27 plain 142, asan 135, tsan 135 of 10799 (reduced runs, not the full suite); phases 17 and 19 plain; gates 33/33; docs 9/9. Next: Day 458.

**Phase 27 Day 456 done (2026-11-18, Linux): components, queries and render components.** `component.get/set/add/remove/has/exists/define` over every stored component of the host's storage registry by name (reads encode and show fields as values; writes build the record, decode it into a scratch component to check it, then record a command, kinds 20 to 22) and `query.each/all/page` (stable id order; `each` keeps the calling partition's entities; at most 60,000 per call). Script-defined components (`ComponentCatalog::Define`: name-ordered layout, id from the name, registered with the new `ComponentRegistry::RegisterDynamic` and `FieldDesc::memory_offset`, saved and loaded by storage). `light_source`, `camera` and `hidden` registered by `scene::RegisterSceneComponents`; `MeshRenderer` waits for Day 463. `/code-review high`: 8 findings, 5 fixed, 3 declined with reasons. Runs: phase 27 plain 131, asan 124, tsan 124 tests, all pass; phase 19 plain 293, asan 267. Next: Day 457.

**Phase 27 Day 455 done (2026-11-18, Linux): entity and transform bindings.** `entity.spawn/destroy/valid/self/name/find/set_parent` and `transform.get/position/rotation/scale/world/set_*/translate` over `LocalTransform`, `WorldTransform` and `Parent`: reads from the tick-start world plus the script's own writes (overlay), writes as merge commands (kinds 10 to 15), spawn returns a usable placeholder; names and prefabs through host-filled `SceneServices` and `MergeServices::prefabs`. A merge command kind can be `global`. Also fixed the three findings of the automated security review of Day 454 (policy on the declared key, no secret writes or notifications, a cut-down script name is not an identity). `/code-review high`: 3 findings (security review of Day 454), fixed with tests. Runs: phase 27 plain 122, asan 115, tsan 115 tests, all pass. Next: Day 456.

**Phase 27 Day 454 done (2026-11-18, Linux): settings bindings.** `settings.get/exists/describe` (pure, tick-start snapshot, secrets refused), `settings.set` (policy allow list, registry validation, a global command applied at the merge), `settings.on_change` (partition subscriptions; `CollectSettingChanges` lists deliveries in partition, subscription, key order); bindings can now raise script errors (`RaiseScriptError`, used by `time.real` too). `/code-review high`: 1 finding (unbounded subscriptions), fixed. Runs: phase 27 plain 113, asan 106, tsan 106 tests, all pass. Next: Day 455.

**Phase 27 Day 453 done (2026-11-18, Linux): math, random, log and time bindings.** `vec2`, `vec4`, `mat4` (16-number array), `quat.slerp/from_euler`, `num.sign/smoothstep/approach`; `random.*` from a per-entity SplitMix64 stream (world seed, stable id, tick: same for any thread count); `log.*` into a per-partition `LogBuffer` flushed in partition order, with the script and line from a backend hook and a per-script limit (`script.log_limit`); `time.dt/now/frame/tick/real` (`real` is nil while a replay records). `/code-review high`: 5 findings, 2 fixed, 3 declined. Runs: plain `ran 3976 of 10685 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3271 of 10685`, tsan `ran 2926 of 10685`; only the known failures. Next: Day 454.

**Phase 27 Day 452 done (2026-11-18, Linux): the world view, command buffers and the ordered merge.** Design A: scripts read the live read-only ECS (`ReadOnlyPhase`), write into per-partition `CommandBuffer`s with reserved placeholder handles and an own-entity `Overlay`, and `Merge` applies them in `(partition, entity key, sequence)` order with a conflict policy (`script.conflicts`). 10,000-entity test: one hash at 1, 2, 7, 16 threads and over 100 runs; a planted race is caught. 23 ns/command to record, 100 ns to merge. `/code-review high`: 3 findings, all fixed. Runs: plain `ran 3953 of 10616 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3248 of 10616`, tsan `ran 2903 of 10616`; only the known failures. Next: Day 453.

**Phase 27 Day 451 done (2026-11-18, Linux): partitions, the state pool and the ownership rules.** `script::ScriptWorld` (`script_world.h`): `P` partitions with lazy states, `PartitionOf` by a fixed hash, one-holder leases with an atomic owner token wired to `State::Acquire/HandOver`, per-partition memory cap with reset, lock-free counters; settings `script.partitions`, `script.partition_memory`. `/code-review high`: 4 findings, all fixed (day file). Runs: plain `ran 3942 of 10583 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3239 of 10589`, tsan `ran 2894 of 10589`; only the known failures. Next: Day 452.

**Phase 27 Day 450 done (2026-11-18, Linux): value marshalling and the binding registry.** `script::BindingRegistry` (`bindings.h`): typed functions become script functions from one declaration (checks, conversions, errors naming the function, stable ids, thread rules, generation-checked entity handles, installs by profile: main, partition, task), standard `num`/`vec3`/`quat` bindings, vector/quaternion operators in the LuaJIT backend, `docs/reference/script-api.md` from `tools/script_api_doc` (ctest `docs_script_api`). Value types stay tagged tables (reason in the day file). Review by hand: see the day file. Runs: plain `ran 3932 of 10553 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3227 of 10553`, tsan `ran 2882 of 10553`; only the known failures. Next: Day 451.

**Phase 27 Day 449 done (2026-11-18, Linux): the sandbox and the budgets.** LuaJIT states are budgeted (JIT off, instruction and time budgets through a count hook, `pcall`/`xpcall`/`coroutine.resume` re-raise a tripped budget) or trusted (JIT on, memory budget only); memory budget per call in both; whitelist environment with read-only engine globals and libraries, `print` to the log, seeded `math.random`; step-limited Lua pattern functions (`lua_patterns.cpp`); settings `script.budget` and `script.random_seed`. Review by hand: see the day file. Runs: plain `ran 3921 of 10522 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3217 of 10522`, tsan `ran 2872 of 10522`; only the known failures (the plain run's `std_usage_check` failure was `unsigned char` in the new file, fixed). Next: Day 450.

**Phase 27 Day 448 done (2026-11-18, Linux): the LuaJIT backend I.** `engine/script/luajit/`: one `lua_State` per state on a counting allocator, calls as coroutines (`wait`, `wait_frames`, `wait_event`), errors as values with traces, natives, tagged value kinds; passes the shared conformance suite (budget cases skipped until Day 449). Review by hand: an out-of-memory coroutine was never dropped (fixed), empty tagged map, huge error messages. Runs: plain `ran 3912 of 10495 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3208 of 10495`, tsan `ran 2863 of 10495`; only the known failures. Next: Day 449 (budgets and the sandbox).

**Phase 27 Day 447 done (2026-11-18, Linux): the second backend and the conformance suite.** `script_expr` (`engine/script/expr/`), the backend registry, setting `script.backend` (default `luajit`), and `tests/script/backend_conformance.h` (12 cases, per-backend kit, planted-fault tests prove it fails the right case). Review by hand: unbounded compiler recursion found and fixed with a nesting limit. Runs: plain `ran 3901 of 10462 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3197 of 10462`, tsan `ran 2852 of 10462`; only the known failures. Next: Day 448 (LuaJIT backend I, which must pass this suite).

**Phase 27 Day 446 done (2026-11-18, Linux): the script module and the backend interface.** `engine/script`: `Value` (a copyable, bounded, hashable value that crosses threads), `Status`/`Outcome`, `Budget`, `Capabilities`, `WaitRequest`, `State` (one owner thread at a time, `HandOver`/`Acquire`) and `Backend`, tested against a scripted fake backend; guide `docs/guide/scripting.md`. Runs: plain `ran 3893 of 10438 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3190 of 10441`, tsan `ran 2845 of 10441`; only the known failures. Next: Day 447 (the second backend and the conformance suite).

**Phase 27 Day 445 done (2026-11-18, Linux): LuaJIT vendored and built, boundary gate.** `third_party/luajit/` (pinned commit, unmodified upstream) builds as the static library `luajit` through CMake's own host-tool steps (`ENGINE_LUAJIT_JIT=OFF` for the interpreter-only build); gate `luajit_boundary_check`; `tests/script/luajit_smoke_test.cpp`. The user accepted the Day 444 recommendations (pin, `P` = 64, world access A, budget mode per world) and Days 449, 452 and 470 are amended. Runs: plain `ran 3874 of 10381 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan and tsan `ran 4 of 10381 tests (phases: 27; ...)`; only the known failures. Linux x86-64 only; Windows and macOS builds are Days 473-474. Next: Day 446.

**Phase 27 Day 444 done (2026-11-18, Linux): scripting design spec and spikes; `CURRENT_PHASE` is 27.** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-18-scripting-design.md`: the same state hash at 1 to 16 threads for `P` = 16, 64, 256 (100,000 scripted entities, 8.2 ms per tick on one thread, 1.5 ms on 16); a state costs 28 KB; 100,000 waiting coroutines 49 MB. Findings that amend the plan: a count-hook budget does not stop JIT-compiled loops (budgeted worlds run interpreter-only), `pcall`/`coroutine.resume` must be replaced so budget errors are not swallowed, `string.find` backtracking is uninterruptible (52 s). LuaJIT pin `63c8f1a9...`. Open: the user's decisions on the pin, `P`, world access A or B and budget versus JIT before Day 445. No product code; nothing built on Windows or macOS.

**C API audio update (2026-11-18, Linux):** `ENGINE_COMPONENT_AUDIO_SOURCE` and `_LISTENER` put sounds on entities (each `frame` places them on the host-driven mixer; `host_audio_world.c`, a `host_cpp.cpp` audio check, the tutorial `audio-in-your-window.md`); `audio_render` is lock-free, `audio_close` no longer frees a mixer a call is inside, `audio_bus_effect_set` retunes instead of using up the 64 effect slots. Not added: occlusion through the physics world for entity sources and device selection (the host owns the device). Scoped runs: plain `ran 3863 of 10362 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; ...)`, asan `ran 3166`, tsan `ran 2821` (the new failures they showed, `engine_c_runtime` and `engine_c_physics`, were two tests using the now-valid mask 0x4000 as an unknown bit, fixed; `std_usage_check` allow-list entry added); remaining failures are the known ones. Not built on Windows or macOS.

The Windows and Mac closing runs are yours. `CURRENT_PHASE` stays 26 until Phase 27 Day 444.
