**Phase 22 plan change (2026-11-14, the user's decision): the glTF importer is an in-memory library (for the host).** `engine/asset_import` (a leaf module no runtime engine module depends on) imports `.glb`/`.gltf` bytes into blobs and adds them to a `HostMemoryBackend`; `asset_mint import-gltf` becomes a thin command over it. **Phase 23 Day 345 follow-up (2026-11-15, Linux, the user's question why two recorded limits were not fixed): they are fixed.** `NotifyThreadEnd` runs a list of functions (`AddThreadEndHook`); the frame-pool cache registers itself on first use and the Vulkan command-pool slot is plain data released there, so no `thread_local` destructor remains on the engine's API path. Threads that record on their own call `NotifyThreadEnd` before they end (the C API's `engine_thread_detach`, Day 346). Runs: plain `ran 3341 of 8864 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded)` (one X11 frame-loop test failed under load and passed alone), asan `ran 2670 of 8864`, tsan `ran 2341 of 8864`, all passed; shared tree `engine_*`/`abi_check*` 50 of 50; Clang and GCC non-unity build clean; `ctest -L docs` 7 of 7.


**Windows PC: known issues fixed or settled on Windows (2026-11-15, out of plan, the user's request).** (1) **Lock detection:** on Windows 11 the lock screen runs on the Default desktop, so `os::QuerySessionState` read a locked session as active a few seconds after Win+L and the clipboard, drag-and-drop and Alt tests failed instead of skipping; it now also reads the session's lock flag (`WTSSessionInfoEx`), and with the screen locked all seven display tests skipped. (2) **The mouse test** checks its posted positions before the real cursor's input is pumped (not tried with a person moving the mouse over it). (3) **BC on the GPU:** new `bc_gpu_test.cpp`; BC7 exact, BC1/4/5 up to two steps from the engine's decoder on the Radeon 860M (the header says one; the decoder's rounding is the user's call, `KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). (4) **The select poller at 1,000 sockets:** 53 us a wait idle, 190 us with all ready, of which `select` is 52 and 187 us (spec section 5); no change kept. The engine's UI runs on a surface with no connection of its own (the program makes one `XOpenDisplay`, the host's). `surface_input_test.cpp` (button click counted through the world API, text, cursor callback, clipboard round trip, UI frame equal to a golden) and `host_surface.c` (validation, overflow, present mode, connection count). **Phase 24 Day 369 done (2026-11-16, the Windows PC): scenes on an `HWND`, Direct3D 12 and Vulkan.** `host_scene.c` and `walk_around.c` reach their window through Day 370's `tests/engine_library/host_window.h`, with a Win32 side (`windows/host_window_win32.c`, added at the rebase onto Day 370), and each scene test runs once per graphics API (`HOST_SURFACE_API`); spikes 1 and 2 open a Win32 window. D3D12 and Vulkan draw the golden scene byte for byte the same here, within 2 of the Linux golden. Found and fixed: D3D12 has one `ID3D12Device` per adapter, so a second surface's destruction reported the first's objects as leaks; and, with the new test hook `surface_lose_device`, `surface_rebind` reused a lost device (no recovery possible), a recovery fell silently to WARP, and a refused rebind dropped the world. A lost device now gets a new one on the surface's adapter. Runs: `ran 3163 of 6085 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded)` (MSVC), `ran 3193 of 3349 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded)` (clang-cl), asan `ran 2639 of 6085 tests (phases: all; flavors: asan; display: included; crash tests: excluded)`; shared `engine_library` 97 of 97 and 85 of 85; docs 7 of 7; only filed flakes failed, each passing 3 of 3 reruns. Runs without the reset-loop test: `ran 3639 of 7033 tests (the `--affected` selection, every phase because the phase touched `cmake/` and `third_party/`, but the phase 8 reset-loop test; flavor: plain; display included; crash tests excluded) in 236 s`, `ran 3109 of 7033 tests (the same selection, flavor asan) in 265 s`, `ran 3669 of 3826 tests (the same selection, flavor plain) in 321 s`; failing only the voice-cost bound in Debug (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`) after the gates learned the new directory. Review: 4 findings, 1 fixed, 3 recorded. Next: Day 441 (the Mac).

**Phase 26 Day 441 done (2026-11-18, the Mac): CoreAudio.** `engine/audio/macos/coreaudio_device.*`: an AUHAL output unit with a render callback, device UIDs as names, the nominal rate and the device's own channel count granted, the wanted buffer size asked of the device, loss and default-output listeners on a private queue drained by `Stop`/`Close`. `audio_probe` builds on macOS and played the tone (47 calls a second, no underrun); the user heard a steady tone. 9 of 9 backend tests and the C API tests pass; the committed rows match Linux's within 5e-7 and the Vorbis rows to six digits; the Mac's same-build hashes are committed (three-value `PlatformHash`). `--affected`: `ran 3682 of 10153 tests (phases: all; flavors: plain; display: included; crash tests: excluded) in 632 s`, failures the flaky jobs throughput test and the Debug voice-cost bound (as on the PC); after the review `ran 222 of 10153` for phase 26. Review: 10 findings, 9 fixed (one regression test shown failing), 1 declined (a shared counter helper across the four backends, for Linux). A locked console stops audio as it stops window tests (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).

**The host locale fix (maintenance, 2026-11-18, the Windows PC, `plans/maintenance/host-locale-independent-import.md`):** a glTF import failed under a host's decimal-comma locale (`materials[0]: a float value is a finite number`): printf's `%.9g` wrote `0,5` into the material text that `ParseDoubleExact` reads back. `FormatGeneral` (`cstr/number.h`, `std::to_chars`, `%.Ng` of the C locale byte for byte, so minted bytes and `import_format_version()` are unchanged) now writes every stored or parsed-back number: the material text, `FormatMaterialBlob`, the import summary, the resampling note, the render settings text (read with `ParseDoubleExact` too), the macOS settings store, `asset_mint`'s level text. `os::SetLocale` and `test::CommaLocale` run the tests under `nb_NO.utf8`; each new test fails without the fix. Runs without the reset-loop test: `build-msvc plain: 3659 of 3661 passed in 268 s`, `build-msvc asan: 3122 of 3123 passed in 280 s`, `build-clang-cl plain: 3689 of 3691 passed in 337 s`; failing the known voice-cost bound and `FramePool` flake, and `test_phase_labels` (phase 27 has no Windows flavor line; `KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Review: 3 findings, 1 fixed (`+-1` accepted by both float parsers), 2 declined. Linux and the Mac have not built it.

**Slot UV transform, the Windows half (2026-11-18, the Windows PC):** `gbuffer.frag`'s DXIL re-minted (`cmake --build build-msvc --target shaders`; no other shader changed); the DXIL test, `engine_c_acceptance_scene` and `engine_c_scene` pass on Direct3D 12 and Vulkan (`build-msvc` plain and asan: 10 of 10; `build-clang-cl` plain: 5 of 5). The Metal library waits for the Mac (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).

**Slot UV transform, the Mac half (2026-11-19, the Mac):** `gbuffer.frag`'s Metal library re-minted (`cmake --build build-macos --target shaders`; no other output changed); the minted-library test and `engine_c_acceptance_scene` pass on Metal with validation (the fitting cases: 4:3 gives 13, 82, 13 columns of bar, picture, bar; 9:16 gives 37, 34, 37, the expected 75% and 31.6% of the screen's width); `engine_c_scene` skipped (the console was locked). The build trees had been deleted from the checkout, so `build-macos` was configured anew.

**Phase 27 Day 473 done (2026-11-19, the Windows PC): LuaJIT, the scripts and the directory watcher on Windows.** LuaJIT builds with MSVC and `clang-cl` through the same CMake route as Linux (DynASM `-D WIN`, `buildvm -m peobj`); the script modules, their tests, the demo, the benchmark and the script API page join the Windows build (plain and asan), and the C API has its script functions there. `io::DirectoryWatcher` has its `ReadDirectoryChangesW` backend (a read Windows cancels when its thread ends is an `Overflow` and a new read: found and fixed with a regression test shown failing without it). Fixed on the way: the no-script `ScriptHost::Impl` was never defined (cl refused the destructor), a C4456 in `host_script.c`, `flavors 27 windows` (`test_phase_labels` passes again), the docs target on Windows. Cross-machine: the C API sample gives Linux's `e3e3ca13a45686f9`; the demo's hash is a committed reference (`0xCD4A4433E698FAD7`, MSVC and clang-cl, Debug and Release, JIT and no JIT) for Linux and the Mac to check. Runs (ctest, every test but the reset loop, crash tests excluded): `build-msvc` plain 3977 of 3980 passed, asan 3439 of 3441, `build-clang-cl` plain 4006 of 4010; failures the known voice-cost bound and Metal library, a stale `docs_check` (regenerated, docs 8 of 8) and three tests that pass alone (`KNOWN-ISSUES.md`); no-JIT tree 249 of 249 script tests; shared trees 95 of 95 and 104 of 104. Eight trees build clean (unity off, shared, importer off). Release demo 28.6 ms of script time at 16 threads (9.1x over 1); a Debug build is far slower (the `/MDd` heap). Review: 5 findings, 2 recorded, 2 no change, 1 declined. **Next: Day 474 (the Mac).**
